# Sofar G3 Inverter — Modbus Protocol Reference

Register map and fault definitions for the Sofar G3 inverter line, converted from a
`Sofar_G3_Modbus.xlsx` spreadsheet. All register addresses are hexadecimal.

## 1. Protocol basics

Standard Modbus RTU over RS-485.

|                         |                                                                 |
| ----------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Device address          | 1–247 (`0x00` = broadcast, write only)                          |
| Read function code      | `0x03`                                                          |
| Write function code     | `0x10`                                                          |
| Exception function code | `0x90`                                                          |
| Checksum                | Modbus CRC-16, transmitted **low byte first**                   |
| Multi-register values   | Big-endian: a 32-bit value is sent `byte3, byte2, byte1, byte0` |

### 1.1 Frame layouts

Each cell below is one byte unless noted; `Hi`/`Lo` denote the high and low byte of a 16-bit field.

**Read request (`0x03`)**

| Device addr | Func   | Start address | Register count | CRC    |
| ----------- | ------ | ------------- | -------------- | ------ |
| 1 byte      | `0x03` | Hi, Lo        | Hi, Lo         | Lo, Hi |

**Read response (`0x03`)**

| Device addr | Func   | Byte count | Register data           | CRC    |
| ----------- | ------ | ---------- | ----------------------- | ------ |
| 1 byte      | `0x03` | 1 byte     | Hi₁, Lo₁ … Hi\_N, Lo\_N | Lo, Hi |

**Write request (`0x10`)**

| Device addr | Func   | Start address | Register count | Byte count | Data                    | CRC    |
| ----------- | ------ | ------------- | -------------- | ---------- | ----------------------- | ------ |
| 1 byte      | `0x10` | Hi, Lo        | Hi, Lo         | 1 byte     | Hi₁, Lo₁ … Hi\_N, Lo\_N | Lo, Hi |

**Write response (`0x10`)**

| Device addr | Func   | Start address | Register count | CRC    |
| ----------- | ------ | ------------- | -------------- | ------ |
| 1 byte      | `0x10` | Hi, Lo        | Hi, Lo         | Lo, Hi |

**Exception response**

| Device addr | Func   | Error code | CRC    |
| ----------- | ------ | ---------- | ------ |
| 1 byte      | `0x90` | 1 byte     | Lo, Hi |

### 1.2 Access flags

| Flag | Meaning                                       |
| ---- | --------------------------------------------- |
| `R`  | Readable                                      |
| `W`  | Writable                                      |
| `V`  | Volatile — supports frequent write operations |

The **Access** column in the register tables (`End User` / `Installer`) indicates the permission
level the register is intended for.

### 1.3 Data types

| Type          | Width        | Notes                                              |
| ------------- | ------------ | -------------------------------------------------- |
| `U16` / `I16` | 1 register   | Unsigned / signed 16-bit                           |
| `U32` / `I32` | 2 registers  | Unsigned / signed 32-bit, big-endian               |
| `U64`         | 4 registers  | Unsigned 64-bit, big-endian                        |
| `BCD16`       | 1 register   | Binary-coded decimal, e.g. `0x0106` = version 1.06 |
| `ASCII`       | 1+ registers | Two characters per register                        |

Where a **Scale** is given, the physical value is `raw × scale` in the listed unit.

---

## 2. Register map

Address ranges spanning several rows indicate a multi-register value. Addresses listed as _reserved_
are defined in the map but carry no documented field.

### General

| Address       | Field                     | Type  | Scale | Unit | Min  | Max   | R/W | Access   | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------- | ------------------------- | ----- | ----- | ---- | ---- | ----- | --- | -------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `0040`–`0043` | `AddressMask_General1`    | U64   | —     | —    | —    | —     | R   | End User | The validity of 64 addresses above the address range (including the address of this partition) corresponding to each bit of this partition.<br>0 means invalid; 1 means valid.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `0044`        | `Modbus_Protocol_Version` | BCD16 | —     | —    | 1.06 | 99.99 | R   | End User | Version number of this protocol. The protocol version number corresponds to the version number in the cover of this protocol.<br>Bits 0-7 represent the minor version number, bits 0-3 represent the low version number, and bits 4-7 represent the high version number. Bits 8-15 represent the major version number, of which bits 8-11 represent the low-order version number, and bits 12-15 represent the high-order version number.<br>Example: The value 0x1234 indicates that the protocol version number is 12.34 |
| `0045`        | `COMM_MCU_Code`           | ASCII | —     | —    | —    | —     | R   | End User | Communication chip code.<br>Refer to the chip coding specification, not open to modify.<br>Example: 0x4730 is the ASCII characters G and 0                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `0046`        | `CTRL1_MCU_Code`          | ASCII | —     | —    | —    | —     | R   | End User | No. 1 control chip code.<br>Refer to the chip coding specification, not open to Modify.<br>Example: 0x4730 is the ASCII characters G and 0                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `0047`        | `CTRL2_MCU_Code`          | ASCII | —     | —    | —    | —     | R   | End User | No. 2 control chip code name.<br>Refer to the chip coding specification, not open to Modify.<br>Example: 0x4730 is the ASCII characters G and 0                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `0048`        | `MCU_Code_Rsvd1`          | —     | —     | —    | —    | —     | —   | —        | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `0049`        | `MCU_Code_Rsvd2`          | —     | —     | —    | —    | —     | —   | —        | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `004A`        | `MCU_Code_Rsvd3`          | —     | —     | —    | —    | —     | —   | —        | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `004B`        | `MCU_Code_Rsvd4`          | —     | —     | —    | —    | —     | —   | —        | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `004C`        | `MCU_Code_Rsvd5`          | —     | —     | —    | —    | —     | —   | —        | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `004D`        | `MCU_Code_Rsvd6`          | —     | —     | —    | —    | —     | —   | —        | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `004E`        | `MCU_Code_Rsvd7`          | —     | —     | —    | —    | —     | —   | —        | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `004F`        | `MCU_Code_Rsvd8`          | —     | —     | —    | —    | —     | —   | —        | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `0050`        | `MCU_Code_Rsvd9`          | —     | —     | —    | —    | —     | —   | —        | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `0051`        | `MCU_Code_Rsvd10`         | —     | —     | —    | —    | —     | —   | —        | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `0052`        | `MCU_Code_Rsvd11`         | —     | —     | —    | —    | —     | —   | —        | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `0053`        | `MCU_Code_Rsvd12`         | —     | —     | —    | —    | —     | —   | —        | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

### Safety Parameter Area(0x0800-0x0FFF)

_Grouping heading — see the subsections below._

#### Boot Parameters (0x0800-0x083F)

| Address | Field                    | Type | Scale | Unit    | Min  | Max   | R/W | Access    | Description                                                                         |
| ------- | ------------------------ | ---- | ----- | ------- | ---- | ----- | --- | --------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `0800`  | `ConnectWaitTime`        | U16  | 1     | Second  | 1    | 65535 | RW  | Installer | Waiting time before generate on grid power                                          |
| `0801`  | `PowerUpSpeed`           | U16  | 1     | %Pn/min | 1    | 3000  | RW  | Installer | 100 mean fully power sided in one minute, Other values are calculated based on this |
| `0802`  | `ReconnectWaitTime`      | U16  | 1     | Second  | 1    | 65535 | RW  | Installer | Waiting time after grid fault recovery                                              |
| `0803`  | `ReconnectPowerUpSpeed`  | U16  | 1     | %Pn/min | 1    | 3000  | RW  | Installer | 100 mean fully power sided in one minute, Other values are calculated based on this |
| `0804`  | `VoltHighLimit`          | U16  | 0.1   | V       | 0    | 65535 | RW  | End User  | Upper limit for starting voltage (Grid side)                                        |
| `0805`  | `VoltLowLimit`           | U16  | 0.1   | V       | 0    | 65535 | RW  | End User  | Lower limit for starting Voltage (Grid Side)                                        |
| `0806`  | `FreqHighLimit`          | U16  | 0.01  | Hz      | 4000 | 7000  | RW  | End User  | Upper limit for starting frequency (Grid side)                                      |
| `0807`  | `FreqLowLimit`           | U16  | 0.01  | Hz      | 4000 | 7000  | RW  | End User  | Lower limit for starting Frequency (Grid Side)                                      |
| `0808`  | `ReconnectVoltHighLimit` | U16  | 0.1   | V       | 0    | 65535 | RW  | End User  | Upper limit for reconnected grid voltage                                            |
| `0809`  | `ReconnectVoltLowLimit`  | U16  | 0.1   | V       | 0    | 65535 | RW  | End User  | Lower limit For reconnected grid voltage                                            |
| `080A`  | `ReconnectFreqHighLimit` | U16  | 0.01  | Hz      | 4000 | 7000  | RW  | End User  | Upper limit for reconnected grid frequency                                          |
| `080B`  | `ReconnectFreqLowLimit`  | U16  | 0.01  | Hz      | 4000 | 7000  | RW  | End User  | Lower limit for reconnected grid frequency                                          |

### Core function configuration (0x2000-0x207F)

| Address       | Field                      | Type  | Scale | Unit | Min | Max | R/W | Access | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------- | -------------------------- | ----- | ----- | ---- | --- | --- | --- | ------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `2000`–`2003` | `AddressMask_Config_Core1` | U64   | —     | —    | —   | —   | R   | —      | Each bit of this field corresponds to the validity of 64 addresses above the address of this field (including the address of this field). Bit4 represents the address where the highest bit address of this field is plus 1.<br>0 means invalid; 1 means valid.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `2004`        | `Country_Code`             | U16   | —     | —    | —   | —   | RW  | —      | Safety country, used for storage                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `2005`        | `Safety_Version`           | U16   | —     | —    | —   | —   | RW  | —      | The version number of the safety parameters of the communication host (upper computer).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `2006`        | `Factory_Reset`            | U16   | —     | —    | 1   | 255 | RW  | —      | Restore factory registers;<br>The bit value is 1 valid.<br>Bit0: Clear battery<br>Bit1: Clear event record<br>Bit2: Restore default values for all function configuration parameters<br>When reading, return the status of the last write operation:<br>0x0000: success<br>0x0001-0x00FF: operating<br>0xFFFB: The operation failed and the controller refused to respond (maybe the controller is busy or the configuration is wrong)<br>0xFFFC: The operation failed and the controller did not respond<br>0xFFFD: Operation failed, current function is forbidden<br>0xFFFE: Operation failed, parameter access failed<br>0xFFFF: The operation failed, the input parameter is wrong |
| `2007`        | `Safety_Name1`             | ASCII | —     | —    | —   | —   | RW  | —      | The first and second place in the name of the safety-regulated country.<br>The upper 8 bits of the register store the first digit of the safety name;<br>The lower 8 bits of the register store the second bit of the safety name.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `2008`        | `Safety_Name2`             | ASCII | —     | —    | —   | —   | RW  | —      | The third and fourth place in the name of the safety-regulated country.<br>The upper 8 bits of the register store the third digit of the safety name;<br>The lower 8 bits of the register store the 4th bit of the safety name.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `2009`        | `Safety_Name3`             | ASCII | —     | —    | —   | —   | RW  | —      | The 5th and 6th place in the name of the safety-regulated country.<br>The upper 8 bits of the register store the 5th digit of the safety name;<br>The lower 8 bits of the register store the 6th bit of the safety name.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `200A`        | `Safety_Name4`             | ASCII | —     | —    | —   | —   | RW  | —      | The 7th and 8th place in the name of the safety-regulated country.<br>The upper 8 bits of the register store the 7th bit of the safety name;<br>The lower 8 bits of the register store the 8th bit of the safety name.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `200B`        | `Safety_Name5`             | ASCII | —     | —    | —   | —   | RW  | —      | The ninth and tenth place in the name of safety regulations.<br>The upper 8 bits of the register store the 9th bit of the safety name;<br>The lower 8 bits of the register store the 10th bit of the safety name.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `200C`–`203C` | _reserved_                 | —     | —     | —    | —   | —   | —   | —      | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `203D`        | `Remote_Upgrade_Control`   | U16   | —     | —    | —   | —   | W   | —      | Remote upgrade control register.<br>High byte. Writing 0xA5 is valid.<br>The low byte contains the chip combination that needs to be upgraded, and the explanation of each bit is as follows:<br>Bit0: communication board;<br>Bit1: control board 1 (main DSP);<br>Bit2: control board 2 (secondary DSP);<br>Bit3: Fuse                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `203E`        | `Local_Upgrade_Control`    | U16   | —     | —    | —   | —   | W   | —      | High byte. Writing 0xA5 is valid.<br>The low byte contains the chip combination that needs to be upgraded, and the explanation of each bit is as follows:<br>Bit0: communication board;<br>Bit1: control board 1 (main DSP);<br>Bit2: control board 2 (secondary DSP);<br>Bit3: Fuse                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `203F`        | `Local_Upgrade_Status`     | U16   | —     | —    | —   | —   | R   | —      | Current upgrade progress<br>The high byte indicates the current target chip to be upgraded:<br>0x00: Not in the upgrade state;<br>0x01: Main DSP upgrade;<br>0x02: Secondary DSP upgrade;<br>0x03: ARM upgrade;<br>0x04: FUSE upgrade;<br>The low byte indicates the current upgrade progress:<br>The progress range is 0-100%                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

### ODM data customization area (0x7000-0x707F)

| Address | Field       | Type  | Scale | Unit | Min | Max | R/W | Access   | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------- | ----------- | ----- | ----- | ---- | --- | --- | --- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `7000`  | _(unnamed)_ | ASCII | —     | —    | —   | —   | RW  | End User | The address of 0x7000-0x707F is a custom area, which must meet the following format:<br>All registers in this area are in ASCII format. The upper 8 bits of the register store front-end characters and the lower 8 bits store back-end characters. For example, when the character string "1234" is stored in 0x7000, 0x7001, the data in 0x7000 is 0x3132; the data in 0x7001 is 0x3334.<br>Allowed characters include: 0x20-0x7E and 0xFF. 0xFF is automatically ignored during parsing.<br>The string format is defined as follows:<br>Key 1=value 1\r\nkey 2=value 2\r\n<br>The keys supported by this protocol version are:<br>"SN": ODM serial number |

---

## 3. Fault and warning bit definitions

Fault registers are read as 16-bit words; each word is split into `byte 0` (low byte) and `byte 1`
(high byte), and each bit flags one condition. Bits not listed are reserved.

Sources: `DSPM` = master DSP, `DSPS` = slave DSP, `DSPM&S` = both, `ARM` = control MCU.

### Fault1: Grid

| Bit        | Flag              | No. | Description                                | Source |
| ---------- | ----------------- | --- | ------------------------------------------ | ------ |
| byte0.bit0 | `GridOVP`         | 001 | Grid over-voltage                          | DSPM   |
| byte0.bit1 | `GridUVP`         | 002 | Grid undervoltage                          | DSPM   |
| byte0.bit2 | `GridOFP`         | 003 | Grid Overfrequency                         | DSPM   |
| byte0.bit3 | `GridUFP`         | 004 | Grid Underfrequency                        | DSPM   |
| byte0.bit4 | `GFCI`            | 005 | Leakage current fault                      | DSPM&S |
| byte0.bit5 | `OVRT`            | 006 | High penetration error                     | DSPM   |
| byte0.bit6 | `LVRT`            | 007 | Low penetration error                      | DSPM   |
| byte0.bit7 | `IslandFault`     | 008 | Islanding error                            | DSPM   |
| byte1.bit0 | `GridOVPInstant1` | 009 | Grid voltage transient value overvoltage 1 | DSPM   |
| byte1.bit1 | `GridOVPInstant2` | 010 | Grid voltage transient value overvoltage 2 | DSPM   |
| byte1.bit2 | `VGridLineFault`  | 011 | Grid line voltage error                    | DSPM   |
| byte1.bit3 | `InvVoltFault`    | 012 | Inverter voltage error                     | DSPM   |
| byte1.bit4 | `RefluxFault`     | 013 | Anti-backflow overload                     | DSPM&S |

## 4. Revision history

**2020-05-23**

- `0x0940` — Add Bit4
- `0x0404` — Add 2 new status descriptions
- `0x09C0` — Add Bit3
- `0x09DB-0x09DE` — Add parameters
- `0x2006` — Update value definition description

**2020-06-03**

- `0x102D` — Add CT automatic calibration register

**2020-06-10**

- `0x0940` — Add Bit5

**2020-06-29**

- `0x0914-0x0915` — Add parameters
- `Fault2-byte1-bit6` — Add fault
- `Fault3-byte1-bit6` — Add fault

**2020-08-31**

- `0x1046` — Add battery protocol type
- `0x1038` — Add address
- `0xA07C-0xA07F` — Add address
- `Fault3-byte0-bit5` — Add InverterSoftStartFail
- `Fault3-byte1-bit7` — Add SNTypeFault
- `Fault9-byte1-bit5` — Add unrecoverSpdFail(DC)
- `Fault9-byte1-bit6` — Add unrecoverSpdFail(AC)
- `Fault2-byte0-bit1` — Modify fault name
- `Fault2-byte1-bit0` — Add HwADFaultDCI(DC)
- `Fault2-byte1-bit1` — Add HwADFaultIdcBranch
- `Fault10-byte1-bit4` — Modify fault name
- `Fault10-byte1-bit5` — Add BMS2CommunicatonFault
- `Fault10-byte1-bit6` — Add BMS3CommunicatonFault
- `Fault10-byte1-bit7` — Add BMS4CommunicatonFault
- `Fault10-byte0-bit7` — Add SafetyVerFault

**2020-09-04**

- `0x803C-0x803F` — Add address

- `0x06D1-0x06E2` — Add address

**2020-10-13**

- `0x0A00` — Added bit field definition to the original address

- `0x0961-0x0962` — Add frequency address of over and under frequency power crossing point

**2021-08-03**

- `0x1098-0x109C` — Add PID configuration address
- `0x110F` — Add PID control address

**2021-08-11**

- `0x044C` — Change from reserved address to valid serial number address
- `0x0470-0x0476` — Added 2 valid serial number addresses, and 5 reserved addresses
- `0x10A0-0x10A1` — Add PLC enable and configuration address
- `0x10A5-0x10A6` — Added BLE enable and configuration address
- `0x10B0-0x10A4` — Modify PID enable and configuration address
- `0x8027` — Change from reserved address to valid serial number address
- `0x8028-0x802E` — Added 2 factory effective serial number addresses, and 5 reserved addresses
- `0x802F-0x8032` — Newly added peripheral configuration address and peripheral configuration BIT
  correspondence table
- `0xA06A` — Add remote reset command
- `Fault12-byte1-bit7` — Added PLC heartbeat failure

**2021-08-31**

- `0x1108` — Change the address data type to signed
- `Fault13、Fault14` — Alarm name change
- `0x0040-0x0043` — Add agreement "general" area
- `0x0044` — Add protocol version number

**2021-09-06**

- `Fault8-byte1-bit0, Fault8-byte1-bit1` — Add lightning protection failure (DC) and lightning
  protection failure (AC)
- `0x0044` — Update protocol version definition
- `0x0045-0x0047` — Add chipset code

**2021-10-11**

- `0x0940` — Add Bit7 definition

**2021-11-03**

- `0x1023` — Add mode definition: phase power mode
- `0x1034` — Language definition
- `0x1061` — Add resonance detection sensitivity register
- `0x1110` — Add mode definition: off-grid mode
- `0x1144-0x1146` — Added off-grid mode register
- `0x203D-0x203F` — Add target chip definition: PCU (Power Control Unit) and BMS (Battery Management
  System)
- `0x7000-0x707F` — Add ODM data customization area
- `ID91` — Add resonance protection ID
- `ID106` — Add serial number model error ID
